Let's design:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # water fill two constraints
    for _ in range(10):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1; add=diff/mask.sum()* (1-h)? Not simply; need add to all until hit 1.
       else:
          mask=h>0; subtract.
```
Efficient projection onto simplex with box: We can solve by adding constant c to h, clipping, and adjusting. The projection of a vector v onto {0≤h≤1, sum=target} is h_i = min(1, max(0, v_i + τ)) for some τ. Find τ by bisection or sort. Since each step, h+momentum not too far. We can implement iterative: add uniform to all, clip; if sum too high low, adjust. For n=400, repeatedly adjusting 200 times okay but can be optimized.
Algorithm:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(v,0,1)
 for _ in range(50):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
      free=(h<1)
      if free.sum()==0: break
      # needed average added among free? We can add diff/free.sum() to free, then clip, repeat.
      h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
   else:
      free=(h>0)
      h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This converges. For 50*400 operations, okay. Could use bisection on τ: find τ such that sum(clip(v+τ,0,1))=target. Since v length 400, bisection 30 iter simpler. Let's implement bisection projection exact. Find lo=min(v)-1? τ range [min(0-target?)]. We can set lo=min(v)-1, hi=max(v). Function sum(clip(v+τ,0,1)) monotone. Bisect 40 times. 40*400=16k per projection; if many steps (10000) 160M, okay. But previous iterative maybe fine. We can implement faster active free adjustment.

Weighted_grad function top-level:
```
def weighted_grad(h,p,n,dx,tol=1e-8):
    G=np.zeros(n)
    idx=np.where(p>tol)[0]
    base=n-1
    for li in idx:
       w=p[li]
       l=li-base
       if l==0:
           G += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
       elif l>0:
           if l<n:
              G[:n-l] += w*(1.0-h[l:])
              G[l:] -= w*h[:n-l]
       else:
           s=-l
           if s<n:
              G[s:] += w*(1.0-h[:n-s])
              G[:n-s] -= w*h[s:]
    G *= dx
    return G
```
For l=0, alternatively slice works; this is correct.

Algorithm refine_gradient:
```
def grad_refine(h,n,deadline,alpha,steps,mu,lr=1.0):
   best=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   mom=np.zeros(n)
   lr_base = 1.0/(alpha*2.0) maybe
   lr=lr_base
   for step in range(steps):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      F=compute_F(h,n)
      m=F.max()
      sh=alpha*(F-m)
      # if alpha huge, underflow; use max
      p=np.exp(sh - sh.max()); p/=p.sum()
      G=weighted_grad(h,p,n,dx,tol=1e-6)
      # optional normalize G, adapt lr
      mom = mu*mom - lr*G
      h_new=project_feasible(h+mom,n)
      # line search? Try if exact c improved, else reduce lr?
      c=compute_c5(h_new,n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=c; best=h_new.copy()
      # accept h_new if smooth_obj decreased? Need compute smooth old maybe.
      # For simplicity, h=h_new; adjust lr based on stall
```

def slsqp_optimize(x0,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline,ftol=1e-13):
    set...

    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_constraint_jac}]

If it calls gradient before objective? It may. We can implement `get_FP(x)` top-level that checks and computes. Then obj/grad call it.
```
def get_FP(x):
  global CACHE_X,CACHE_F,CACHE_P
  if CACHE_X is not None and CACHE_X.shape==x.shape and np.array_equal(CACHE_X,x): return CACHE_F,CACHE_P
  n=OBJ_N; dx=OBJ_DX
  F=compute_F(x,n,dx)
  m=np.max(F)
  sh=OBJ_ALPHA*(F-m)
  sm=np.max(sh)
  p=np.exp(sh-sm); p/=p.sum()
  CACHE_X=np.array(x,copy=True); CACHE_F=F; CACHE_P=p
  return F,p
```

def polish_sequence(h,n,deadline,alphas_iters):
  cur=h.copy(); cur_c=compute_c5(cur,n)
  for alpha,maxit in alphas:
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    new=slsqp_optimize(cur,n,alpha,maxit)
    new=project(new)
    nc=compute_c5(new)
    if nc < cur_c:
       cur=new; cur_c=nc
    # maybe if no improvement, still proceed
  return cur,cur_c
```

Use bisection to guarantee sum. But every SLSQP final projection maybe many? Only a few. For gradient, use many; maybe need faster. Could implement an iterative free-adjust (few loops) and fallback bisection if diff not small:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            mask=h<1.0
            if not mask.any(): break
            h[mask]+=diff/float(mask.sum())
        else:
            mask=h>0.0
            if not mask.any(): break
            h[mask]+=diff/float(mask.sum())
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # if still off, bisection 40
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
       lo=min(h.min()-1.0, -1.0); hi=max(h.max()+1.0,1.0)
       for _ in range(50):
          mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
          s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
          if s<target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       h=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    return h
```
This adjusts h itself during bisection? If h already clipped, adding mid maybe. But target may require shifting. Better for bisection use original v clipped? We can just use v not h. But h after iterative close. Simpler: if diff fail, use original clipped `base=np.clip(v,0,1)`, bisection. Need not overcomplicate.
The iterative addition may overflow to >1; clip at end. It may not converge if many saturate; bisection fallback.

def logsumexp_value(F, alpha):
   m=F.max()
   sh=alpha*(F-m)
   sm=sh.max() # 0
   return m + (sm + math.log(np.exp(sh-sm).sum()))/alpha
```

```

def set_slsqp(n, mode, alpha=1.0, beta=0.0, m=0.0):
  global CUR_N,CUR_DX,CUR_TARGET,OBJ_MODE,OBJ_ALPHA,FEAS_BETA,FEAS_M
  CUR_N=n; CUR_DX=2.0/n; CUR_TARGET=0.5*n; OBJ_MODE=mode; OBJ_ALPHA=max(1.0,alpha); FEAS_BETA=max(1.0,beta); FEAS_M=m
```

`rc_obj` uses `CUR_N`, `CUR_DX`. It should not use cache maybe.
```
def rc_obj(x):
 F=corr_f(x)
 if OBJ_MODE==0: return lse(F,OBJ_ALPHA)
 else:
   z=FEAS_BETA*(F-FEAS_M)
   # stable log1p(exp)
   return float(np.mean(np.logaddexp(0.0,z)))
```
`np.logaddexp` stable. For large positive z, mean large; for negative, ~exp. Good.

`rc_grad`:
```
def rc_grad(x):
 F=corr_f(x)
 if OBJ_MODE==0:
   p=softmax(F,OBJ_ALPHA)
   return weighted_grad(x,p,CUR_N,CUR_DX,1e-14)
 else:
   z=FEAS_BETA*(F-FEAS_M)
   q=0.5*(1.0+np.tanh(0.5*z))
   g=weighted_grad(x,q,CUR_N,CUR_DX,0.0)
   return g*(FEAS_BETA/float(F.shape[0]))
```
For max mode, p sum 1; for feas, q sum not 1; gradient correct. `weighted_grad` uses p weights as given; for feas q sum arbitrary.

Potential numerical: z negative large -> q=0. Good.

CUR_N=0; CUR_DX=0.0; CUR_TARGET=0.0; OBJ_MODE=0; OBJ_ALPHA=1.0; FEAS_BETA=1.0; FEAS_M=0.0

def _correlate_f(h):
 n=CUR_N; dx=CUR_DX
 return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx

def compute_c5(h,n,dx=None):
 if dx is None: dx=2.0/n
 c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
 return float(np.max(c))

`weighted_grad`:
```
def weighted_grad(h,p,n,dx,tol=0.0):
 G=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
 base=n-1
 idx=np.flatnonzero(p>tol)
 for li in idx:
   w=p[li]
   l=base-li
   if l==0: G += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
   elif l>0:
      G[:n-l] += w*(1.0-h[l:])
      G[l:] -= w*h[:n-l]
   else:
      s=-l
      G[s:] += w*(1.0-h[:n-s])
      G[:n-s] -= w*h[s:]
 G *= dx
 return G
```
Need if l positive >n? lags max n-1. okay.

`softmax_weights(F,alpha)`:
```
def softmax_weights(F,alpha):
 m=F.max(); sh=alpha*(F-m); sh=sh-sh.max(); p=np.exp(sh); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-300: p[F.argmax()]=1 else p/=s; return p
```
`lse_value`:
```
def lse_value(F,alpha):
 m=F.max(); sh=alpha*(F-m); sh=sh-sh.max(); return float(m + (sh.max()+math.log(np.exp(sh).sum()))/alpha)
```
Since sh max 0.

`softplus_value` and `sigmoid_weights`:
Use `np.logaddexp(0.0,z)`. For gradient: `q=0.5*(1+np.tanh(0.5*z))`.

def sum_constraint(x): return float(np.sum(x)-CUR_TARGET)

def polish_slsqp(x,n,mode,alpha=1000.0,beta=0.0,m=0.0,maxiter=300):
 set_slsqp...
 try:
   res=minimize(rc_obj,x.copy(),jac=rc_grad,method='SLSQP',constraints=cons,bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-15,'iprint':0})
   if res.success or ...: x2=res.x

 return project_feasible(x2,n)

Potential problem: `project_feasible` iterative modifies h and then bisection fallback with base? Let's code robust:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
 target=0.5*n
 h=np.clip(v,0,1)
 for _ in range(80):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
      mask=h<1.0
      cnt=int(mask.sum())
      if cnt==0: break
      h[mask]+=diff/cnt
   else:
      mask=h>0.0
      cnt=int(mask.sum())
      if cnt==0: break
      h[mask]+=diff/cnt
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-9:
   base=np.clip(v,0,1)
   lo=base.min()-1.0
   hi=base.max()+1.0
   # ensure lo too low, hi enough: sum at lo < target, at hi > target
   for _ in range(80):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s=np.clip(base+mid,0,1).sum()
      if s<target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   h=np.clip(base+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
 return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

CUR_N = 0
CUR_DX = 0.0
CUR_TARGET = 0.0
OBJ_MODE = 0
OBJ_ALPHA = 1.0
FEAS_BETA = 1.0
FEAS_M = 0.0


def _correlate_float(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * CUR_DX


def _compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
    return float(np.max(c))


def _enforce_exact_sum(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(n):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            d = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
            h[i] += d
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            d = min(-diff, h[i])
            h[i] -= d
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_feasible(v, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - target) > 1e-10:
        base = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
        lo = float(np.min(base)) - 1.0
        hi = float(np.max(base)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(80):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = float(np.sum(np.clip(base + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
            if s < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(base + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    return _enforce_exact_sum(h, n)


def resample_h(h, n, nn):
    if nn == n:
        return np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    y = (np.arange(nn, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(nn))
    return np.interp(y, x, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))


def weighted_grad(h, p, n, dx, tol=0.0):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    base = n - 1
    idx = np.flatnonzero(p > tol)
    for li in idx:
        w = float(p[li])
        l = base - int(li)
        if l == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif l > 0:
            g[:n - l] += w * (1.0 - h[l:])
            g[l:] -= w * h[:n - l]
        else:
            s = -l
            g[s:] += w * (1.0 - h[:n - s])
            g[:n - s] -= w * h[s:]
    g *= dx
    return g


def softmax_weights(f, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(f))
    sh = alpha * (f - m)
    sh = sh - float(np.max(sh))
    p = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s < 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(f)
        p[int(np.argmax(f))] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / s


def logistic_weights(f, beta, m):
    z = beta * (f - m)
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + np.tanh(0.5 * z))


def _set_slsqp(n, mode, alpha, beta, m):
    global CUR_N, CUR_DX, CUR_TARGET, OBJ_MODE, OBJ_ALPHA, FEAS_BETA, FEAS_M
    CUR_N = n
    CUR_DX = 2.0 / float(n)
    CUR_TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    OBJ_MODE = mode
    OBJ_ALPHA = max(1.0, float(alpha))
    FEAS_BETA = max(1.0, float(beta))
    FEAS_M = float(m)


def sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - CUR_TARGET)


def sum_constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(x)


def slsqp_obj(x):
    f = _correlate_float(x)
    if OBJ_MODE == 0:
        m = float(np.max(f))
        sh = OBJ_ALPHA * (f - m)
        sh = sh - float(np.max(sh))
        val = m + (0.0 + math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(sh))))) / OBJ_ALPHA
        return float(val)
    z = FEAS_BETA * (f - FEAS_M)
    return float(np.mean(np.logaddexp(0.0, z)))


def slsqp_grad(x):
    n = CUR_N
    f = _correlate_float(x)
    if OBJ_MODE == 0:
        p = softmax_weights(f, OBJ_ALPHA)
        return weighted_grad(x, p, n, CUR_DX, 1e-15)
    q = logistic_weights(f, FEAS_BETA, FEAS_M)
    return weighted_grad(x, q, n, CUR_DX, 0.0) * (FEAS_BETA / float(f.shape[0]))


def slsqp_polish(x0, n, mode, alpha, beta, m, maxiter):
    _set_slsqp(n, mode, alpha, beta, m)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "jac": sum_constraint_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_obj,
            np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy(),
            jac=slsqp_grad,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(max(10, maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-14, "iprint": 0},
        )
        x = res.x
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
    return project_feasible(x, n)


def try_polish(h, n, c, mode, alpha, beta, m, maxiter):
    h2 = slsqp_polish(h, n, mode, alpha, beta, m, maxiter)
    c2 = _compute_c5(h2, n)
    if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < c - 1e-15:
        return h2, c2
    return h, c


def add_candidate(cands, h, n):
    hp = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(hp)):
        return
    c = _compute_c5(hp, n)
    if np.isfinite(c):
        cands.append((c, len(cands), hp))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        hb = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(hb))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0, endpoint=False) + 1.0 / n0
        hb = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.1 * rng.randn(n0)

    if n0 < 100:
        n1 = min(400, max(2 * n0, 200))
        hb = resample_h(hb, n0, n1)
        n0 = n1
    elif n0 > 800:
        hb = resample_h(hb, n0, 800)
        n0 = 800

    n = n0
    hb = project_feasible(hb, n)

    cands = []
    add_candidate(cands, hb, n)
    add_candidate(cands, hb[::-1], n)
    add_candidate(cands, 1.0 - hb[::-1], n)
    add_candidate(cands, 0.5 * (hb + hb[::-1]), n)
    add_candidate(cands, 0.5 * (hb + 1.0 - hb[::-1]), n)

    if not cands:
        n = 400
        hbest = project_feasible(0.5 + 0.2 * np.sin(np.linspace(0, 4 * np.pi, n)), n)
        cbest = _compute_c5(hbest, n)
        return hbest, cbest, n

    cands.sort()
    best_h, best_c = cands[0][2].copy(), cands[0][0]

    if budget_s > 120.0:
        alphas = [(800.0, 220), (8000.0, 180), (80000.0, 120)]
        for item in cands[:3]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            c0 = item[0]
            if c0 > best_c + 5e-4:
                break
            hh = item[2].copy()
            cc = c0
            for alpha, iters in alphas:
                if time.time() > deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                hh, cc = try_polish(hh, n, cc, 0, alpha, 0.0, 0.0, iters)
                if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()

    if budget_s > 320.0 and time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        dx = 2.0 / float(n)
        targets = []
        targets.append(min(0.38078, best_c - 3e-6))
        targets.append(min(0.38074, best_c - 1.0e-5))
        targets.append(best_c - 2.5e-5)

        for tgt in targets:
            if time.time() > deadline - 100.0:
                break
            if not (tgt < best_c - 1e-8):
                continue
            m = float(tgt) / dx
            for beta, iters in [(700.0, 200), (3500.0, 160)]:
                if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                    break
                best_h, best_c = try_polish(
                    best_h, n, best_c, 1, 1.0, beta, m, iters
                )
            if best_c <= 0.38078:
                break

    if budget_s > 420.0 and time.time() < deadline - 160.0:
        for alpha, iters in [(20000.0, 120), (200000.0, 80)]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 80.0:
                break
            best_h, best_c = try_polish(best_h, n, best_c, 0, alpha, 0.0, 0.0, iters)

    if n < 800 and time.time() < deadline - 320.0:
        n2 = min(1000, max(800, 2 * n))
        h2 = project_feasible(resample_h(best_h, n, n2), n2)
        c2 = _compute_c5(h2, n2)
        if c2 <= best_c + 5e-4:
            for alpha, iters in [(2000.0, 140), (20000.0, 100)]:
                if time.time() > deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                h2, c2 = try_polish(h2, n2, c2, 0, alpha, 0.0, 0.0, iters)
                if c2 <= 0.38078:
                    break
            if time.time() < deadline - 120.0 and c2 > 0.38078:
                m = float(0.38077) / (2.0 / float(n2))
                h2, c2 = try_polish(h2, n2, c2, 1, 1.0, 2000.0, m, 140)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h, best_c, n = h2, c2, n2

    if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_polish(best_h, n, best_c, 0, 50000.0, 0.0, 0.0, 80)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = _compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n)
```
